## Education Courses

- [Drawing a basic 2D bridge](https://www.bentley.com/education-course/drawing-a-basic-2d-bridge/) - In this course, you will follow along with video tutorials to learn how to use MicroStation to create three different views of a 2D (two-dimensional) bridge (no certificate of completion). This course was created for the AASHTO STEM Outreach Bridge Challenge, but can be used by anyone to create a basic bridge drawing.
- [GeoStudio: Slope/W Fundamentals](https://www.bentley.com/education-course/geostudio-slope-w-fundamentals/)
- [PLAXIS 3D: Getting Started](https://www.bentley.com/education-course/plaxis-3d-getting-started/) - This course will explore how you can define complex soil profiles and geological cross-sections, structural elements, such as piles, anchors, geotextiles, and prescribed loads and displacements as well as automatically generating the Finite Element mesh and running staged construction calculations.
